blender_importer = '''
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import bpy
from mathutils import Vector

JSON_PATH = Path("/ABSOLUTE/PATH/TO/archives_ground.json")

def clear_scene():
    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action='SELECT')
    bpy.ops.object.delete(use_global=False)

def ensure_collection(name, parent=None):
    if name in bpy.data.collections:
        return bpy.data.collections[name]
    col = bpy.data.collections.new(name)
    if parent is None:
        bpy.context.scene.collection.children.link(col)
    else:
        parent.children.link(col)
    return col

def load_data(path):
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text())

def point_lookup(data):
    return {p["id"]: Vector((p["x"], p["y"], 0.0)) for p in data.get("points", [])}

def make_floor_slab(name, footprint, z, thickness, collection):
    mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name + "_mesh")
    obj = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)
    collection.objects.link(obj)
    verts = [(p["x"], p["y"], z) for p in footprint]
    faces = [tuple(range(len(verts)))]
    mesh.from_pydata(verts, [], faces)
    mesh.update()
    bpy.context.view_layer.objects.active = obj
    obj.select_set(True)
    bpy.ops.object.mode_set(mode='EDIT')
    bpy.ops.mesh.extrude_region_move(TRANSFORM_OT_translate={"value": (0, 0, thickness)})
    bpy.ops.object.mode_set(mode='OBJECT')
    obj.select_set(False)
    return obj

def build_wall_segment(name, a, b, thickness, height, z, collection):
    direction = (b - a)
    length = direction.length
    center = (a + b) / 2
    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(location=(center.x, center.y, z + height / 2))
    obj = bpy.context.active_object
    obj.name = name
    obj.scale = (length / 2, thickness / 2, height / 2)
    angle = math.atan2(direction.y, direction.x)
    obj.rotation_euler[2] = angle
    collection.objects.link(obj)
    bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.unlink(obj)
    return obj

def build_walls(data, collection):
    pts = point_lookup(data)
    defaults = data["defaults"]
    z = data["levels"]["floor_z"] + data["levels"]["slab_thickness"]
    for wall in data["walls"]:
        a = pts[wall["a"]]
        b = pts[wall["b"]]
        thickness = wall.get("thickness", defaults["wall_thickness"])
        build_wall_segment(wall["id"], a, b, thickness, defaults["wall_height"], z, collection)

def build_room_markers(data, collection):
    for room in data["rooms"]:
        poly = room["polygon"]
        xs = [p["x"] for p in poly]
        ys = [p["y"] for p in poly]
        cx = sum(xs) / len(xs)
        cy = sum(ys) / len(ys)
        bpy.ops.object.empty_add(type='PLAIN_AXES', location=(cx, cy, data["levels"]["floor_z"] + 0.2))
        obj = bpy.context.active_object
        obj.name = f'ROOM_{room["id"]}_{room["name"]}'
        collection.objects.link(obj)
        bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.unlink(obj)

def build_stairs(data, collection):
    z = data["levels"]["floor_z"] + data["levels"]["slab_thickness"]
    for stair in data["stairs"]:
        bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(location=(stair["x"], stair["y"], z + 0.15))
        obj = bpy.context.active_object
        obj.name = stair["id"]
        obj.scale = (stair["run_length"]/2, stair["width"]/2, 0.15)
        collection.objects.link(obj)
        bpy.context.scene.collection.objects.unlink(obj)

def main():
    clear_scene()
    data = load_data(JSON_PATH)
    building_col = ensure_collection(data["building"])
    floor_col = ensure_collection(data["floor"], building_col)
    make_floor_slab(f'{data["building"]}_{data["floor"]}_slab', data["footprint"], data["levels"]["floor_z"], data["levels"]["slab_thickness"], floor_col)
    build_walls(data, floor_col)
    build_stairs(data, floor_col)
    build_room_markers(data, floor_col)
    print(f'Imported {data["id"]}')

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''.strip()
(blender_dir / 'import_floor_json.py').write_text(blender_importer, encoding='utf-8')

build_export = '''
import json
from pathlib import Path
import bpy

FLOOR_DIR = Path("/ABSOLUTE/PATH/TO/floors")
EXPORT_DIR = Path("/ABSOLUTE/PATH/TO/models")
BUILDING_NAME = "Archives"

def export_glb(building_name):
    EXPORT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    filepath = EXPORT_DIR / f"{building_name.lower()}.glb"
    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(filepath=str(filepath), export_format='GLB')
    print(f"Exported {filepath}")

# Intended use:
# 1. Import all floor JSONs for one building into the same Blender scene.
# 2. Verify stair alignment and vertical stacking.
# 3. Export a single GLB per building.
'''.strip()
(blender_dir / 'build_and_export.py').write_text(build_export, encoding='utf-8')

validator = '''
import json
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict

FLOOR_DIR = Path("./examples")

def load_jsons():
    return [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in FLOOR_DIR.glob("*.json")]

def validate_ids(data):
    seen = set()
    problems = []
    for group in ["walls", "doors", "stairs", "rooms", "hotspots"]:
        for item in data.get(group, []):
            item_id = item["id"]
            if item_id in seen:
                problems.append(f"Duplicate id: {item_id}")
            seen.add(item_id)
    return problems

def validate_door_wall_refs(data):
    wall_ids = {w["id"] for w in data["walls"]}
    problems = []
    for d in data["doors"]:
        if d["wall_id"] not in wall_ids:
            problems.append(f'Door {d["id"]} references missing wall {d["wall_id"]}')
    return problems

def validate_stair_alignment(datas):
    by_building = defaultdict(list)
    for d in datas:
        by_building[d["building"]].append(d)
    problems = []
    for building, floors in by_building.items():
        names = defaultdict(list)
        for f in floors:
            for s in f.get("stairs", []):
                names[s["name"]].append((f["floor"], round(s["x"], 2), round(s["y"], 2)))
        for stair_name, uses in names.items():
            xs = {u[1] for u in uses}
            ys = {u[2] for u in uses}
            if len(uses) > 1 and (len(xs) > 1 or len(ys) > 1):
                problems.append(f"{building}: stair '{stair_name}' misaligned across floors: {uses}")
    return problems

def main():
    datas = load_jsons()
    problems = []
    for d in datas:
        problems.extend([f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_ids(d)])
        problems.extend([f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_door_wall_refs(d)])
    problems.extend(validate_stair_alignment(datas))
    if problems:
        print("VALIDATION PROBLEMS:")
        for p in problems:
            print("-", p)
    else:
        print("All validations passed.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''.strip()
(tools_dir / 'validate_floor_data.py').write_text(validator, encoding='utf-8')

godot_loader = '''
extends Node3D

@export var buildings_manifest_path: String = "res://data/buildings.json"

func _ready() -> void:
	var data = _load_json(buildings_manifest_path)
	if data.is_empty():
		push_error("No building manifest loaded.")
		return

	for building in data.get("buildings", []):
		_spawn_building(building)

func _load_json(path: String) -> Dictionary:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		return {}
	var f = FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)
	var text = f.get_as_text()
	var parsed = JSON.parse_string(text)
	if typeof(parsed) == TYPE_DICTIONARY:
		return parsed
	return {}

func _spawn_building(building: Dictionary) -> void:
	var glb_path = building.get("glb", "")
	if glb_path == "":
		return
	var scene_res = load(glb_path)
	if scene_res == null:
		push_warning("Could not load GLB: %s" % glb_path)
		return
	var inst = scene_res.instantiate()
	inst.name = building.get("name", "Building")
	var pos = building.get("position", [0,0,0])
	inst.position = Vector3(pos[0], pos[1], pos[2])
	inst.rotation_degrees = Vector3(0, building.get("rotation_deg", 0), 0)
	add_child(inst)
'''.strip()
(godot_dir / 'building_loader.gd').write_text(godot_loader, encoding='utf-8')

func interact() -> void:

(godot_dir / 'hotspot.gd').write_text(hotspot, encoding='utf-8')

player = '''
extends CharacterBody3D

@onready var head: Node3D = $Head

func _ready() -> void:
	Input.set_mouse_mode(Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED)

func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseMotion:
		rotate_y(-event.relative.x * mouse_sensitivity)
		head.rotate_x(-event.relative.y * mouse_sensitivity)
		head.rotation.x = clamp(head.rotation.x, deg_to_rad(-89), deg_to_rad(89))

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	var input_dir := Input.get_vector("move_left", "move_right", "move_forward", "move_back")
	var basis := global_transform.basis
	var dir := (basis.x * input_dir.x + basis.z * input_dir.y).normalized()
	velocity.x = dir.x * speed
	velocity.z = dir.z * speed
	if not is_on_floor():
		velocity += get_gravity() * delta
	move_and_slide()
'''.strip()

Audit your current prompt collection for canon drift, duplicate naming, and legacy contradictions.

Create a master backlog of all remaining building/floor/room JSON files and all remaining 2D renders still needed.

Create a phase plan:

MVP

first playable build

canon polish pass

atmosphere pass

web viewer pass

Write the README / build instructions / repo conventions for the whole project.

Write the Copilot / Codex prompts for each next implementation step in VS Code.

If you want, I can also produce the next real deliverables, not just advice:

the next 3–5 JSON files

the reconstruction standards file

the campus/building manifests

the upgraded validator

the next Godot UI scripts

Short version
The biggest ChatGPT-doable work still left is:

more canonical data files

better automation scripts

better validation

real Godot UI / loader systems

project-wide standards and manifests

That’s the bulk of the “AI for structure and glue” side of the pipeline you described. 


The best next move is for me to do these four things next, in order:

hollows_ground.json

hollows_basement.json

medica_ground.json

reconstruction_defaults.json

Those would make the pipeline feel real rather than just scaffolded.


Great do all that 



Analyzing
from pathlib import Path
import json, textwrap, math, os

base = Path('/mnt/data/university_pipeline_starter')
base.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
examples_dir = base / 'examples'
tools_dir = base / 'tools'
godot_dir = base / 'godot'
config_dir = base / 'config'
data_dir = godot_dir / 'data'
for d in [examples_dir, tools_dir, godot_dir, config_dir, data_dir]:
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Shared defaults
reconstruction_defaults = {
    "units": "m",
    "north_up": True,
    "global_conventions": {
        "public_institutional_floor_height": 4.6,
        "ordinary_upper_floor_height": 3.8,
        "basement_floor_height": 3.5,
        "utility_floor_height": 3.2,
        "slab_thickness": 0.35,
        "default_wall_thickness_exterior": 0.65,
        "default_wall_thickness_interior": 0.35,
        "default_door_height": 2.2,
        "default_door_width_single": 1.0,
        "default_door_width_double": 1.8,
        "default_window_sill_height": 1.1,
        "stair_riser": 0.17,
        "stair_tread": 0.28
    },
    "certainty_tags": {
        "canon": "directly supported by the books",
        "implied": "strongly supported inference",
        "reconstructed": "practical fill where canon is silent"
    },
    "modeling_rules": [
        "Do not derive 3D directly from raster plans alone; use approved floor JSON as source of truth.",
        "Keep north aligned across all floors and buildings.",
        "Align stairs vertically unless canon explicitly requires mismatch.",
        "Exterior shells should not be exceeded by interior or basement footprints unless explicitly supported.",
        "Use game mesh first, hero mesh later."
    ]
}
(config_dir / 'reconstruction_defaults.json').write_text(json.dumps(reconstruction_defaults, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

            ]
        }
    ]

(data_dir / 'buildings.json').write_text(json.dumps(buildings_manifest, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

# Helper for example data creation
def save_example(name, data):
    (examples_dir / f"{name}.json").write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    # copy to Godot data/floors for convenience
    floors_dir = data_dir / 'floors'
    floors_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (floors_dir / f"{name}.json").write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

# Upgrade validator
validator_v2 = '''
import json
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict

FLOOR_DIR = Path("./examples")

def load_jsons():
    return [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in FLOOR_DIR.glob("*.json")]

def point_map(data):
    return {p["id"]: (p["x"], p["y"]) for p in data.get("points", [])}

def validate_ids(data):
    seen = set()
    problems = []
    for group in ["points", "walls", "doors", "stairs", "windows", "rooms", "hotspots"]:
        for item in data.get(group, []):
            item_id = item["id"]
            if item_id in seen:
                problems.append(f"Duplicate id: {item_id}")
            seen.add(item_id)
    return problems

def validate_refs(data):
    problems = []
    points = {p["id"] for p in data.get("points", [])}
    walls = {w["id"] for w in data.get("walls", [])}
    rooms = {r["id"] for r in data.get("rooms", [])}
    for w in data.get("walls", []):
        if w["a"] not in points or w["b"] not in points:
            problems.append(f'Wall {w["id"]} references missing point')
    for d in data.get("doors", []):
        if d["wall_id"] not in walls:
            problems.append(f'Door {d["id"]} references missing wall {d["wall_id"]}')
    for w in data.get("windows", []):
        if w["wall_id"] not in walls:
            problems.append(f'Window {w["id"]} references missing wall {w["wall_id"]}')
    for h in data.get("hotspots", []):
        if h["room_id"] not in rooms:
            problems.append(f'Hotspot {h["id"]} references missing room {h["room_id"]}')
    return problems

def polygon_bbox(poly):
    xs = [p["x"] for p in poly]
    ys = [p["y"] for p in poly]
    return min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys)

def validate_rooms_within_footprint(data):
    problems = []
    fx1, fy1, fx2, fy2 = polygon_bbox(data["footprint"])
    for room in data.get("rooms", []):
        rx1, ry1, rx2, ry2 = polygon_bbox(room["polygon"])
        if rx1 < fx1 or ry1 < fy1 or rx2 > fx2 or ry2 > fy2:
            problems.append(f'Room {room["id"]} exceeds footprint bounds')
    return problems

def validate_stair_alignment(datas):
    by_building = defaultdict(list)
    for d in datas:
        by_building[d["building"]].append(d)
    problems = []
    for building, floors in by_building.items():
        by_name = defaultdict(list)
        for f in floors:
            for s in f.get("stairs", []):
                by_name[s["name"]].append((f["floor"], round(s["x"], 2), round(s["y"], 2)))
        for stair_name, items in by_name.items():
            if len(items) > 1:
                xs = {i[1] for i in items}
                ys = {i[2] for i in items}
                if len(xs) > 1 or len(ys) > 1:
                    problems.append(f"{building}: stair '{stair_name}' misaligned across floors: {items}")
    return problems

def validate_basement_footprint(datas):
    by_building = defaultdict(list)
    for d in datas:
        by_building[d["building"]].append(d)
    problems = []
    for building, floors in by_building.items():
        grounds = [f for f in floors if f["story_role"] == "ground"]
        basements = [f for f in floors if f["story_role"] == "basement"]
        if grounds and basements:
            gx1, gy1, gx2, gy2 = polygon_bbox(grounds[0]["footprint"])
            for b in basements:
                bx1, by1, bx2, by2 = polygon_bbox(b["footprint"])
                if bx1 < gx1 or by1 < gy1 or bx2 > gx2 or by2 > gy2:
                    problems.append(f"{building}: basement footprint exceeds ground footprint")
    return problems

def main():
    datas = load_jsons()
    problems = []
    for d in datas:
        problems.extend([f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_ids(d)])
        problems.extend([f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_refs(d)])
        problems.extend([f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_rooms_within_footprint(d)])
    problems.extend(validate_stair_alignment(datas))
    problems.extend(validate_basement_footprint(datas))
    if problems:
        print("VALIDATION PROBLEMS:")
        for p in problems:
            print("-", p)
    else:
        print("All validations passed.")
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''.strip()
(tools_dir / 'validate_floor_data_v2.py').write_text(validator_v2, encoding='utf-8')

# Godot UI/manager scaffolds
floor_visibility = '''
extends Node

var current_building_id: String = ""
var current_floor_id: String = ""

func show_only_floor(building_root: Node, floor_name: String) -> void:
    for child in building_root.get_children():
        child.visible = (child.name == floor_name)

func show_all_floors(building_root: Node) -> void:
    for child in building_root.get_children():
        child.visible = true
'''.strip()
(godot_dir / 'floor_visibility_manager.gd').write_text(floor_visibility, encoding='utf-8')

hotspot_panel = '''
extends Control

@onready var title_label = $MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/Title
@onready var meta_label = $MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/Meta
@onready var text_label = $MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/Text

func show_hotspot(data: Dictionary) -> void:
    visible = true
    title_label.text = data.get("title", "")
    meta_label.text = "%s | %s | %s" % [data.get("building", ""), data.get("floor", ""), data.get("certainty", "")]
    text_label.text = data.get("lore_text", "")

func hide_panel() -> void:
    visible = false
'''.strip()
(godot_dir / 'hotspot_panel.gd').write_text(hotspot_panel, encoding='utf-8')

building_floor_selector = '''
extends Control

signal building_selected(building_id: String)
signal floor_selected(floor_id: String)

func populate_buildings(manifest: Dictionary) -> void:
    # Hook this up to OptionButtons in your scene.
    pass

func emit_building(id: String) -> void:
    emit_signal("building_selected", id)

func emit_floor(id: String) -> void:
    emit_signal("floor_selected", id)
'''.strip()
(godot_dir / 'building_floor_selector.gd').write_text(building_floor_selector, encoding='utf-8')

compass = '''
extends Control

@export var player_path: NodePath
var player: Node3D

func _ready() -> void:
    if player_path != NodePath():
        player = get_node(player_path)

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
    if player:
        rotation = -player.rotation.y
'''.strip()
(godot_dir / 'north_compass.gd').write_text(compass, encoding='utf-8')

overlay = '''
extends Sprite2D

@export var floor_texture: Texture2D

func toggle_visible() -> void:
    visible = !visible
'''.strip()
(godot_dir / 'floor_overlay.gd').write_text(overlay, encoding='utf-8')

from pathlib import Path
import json

base = Path('/mnt/data/university_pipeline_starter')
examples_dir = base / 'examples'
tools_dir = base / 'tools'
godot_dir = base / 'godot'

    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    "units": "m",
    "north_up": True,
    "global_conventions": {
        "public_institutional_floor_height": 4.6,
        "ordinary_upper_floor_height": 3.8,
        "basement_floor_height": 3.5,
        "utility_floor_height": 3.2,
        "slab_thickness": 0.35,

        "default_wall_thickness_interior": 0.35,
        "default_door_height": 2.2,
        "default_door_width_single": 1.0,
        "default_door_width_double": 1.8,
        "stair_riser": 0.17,
        "stair_tread": 0.28
    },
    "certainty_tags": {
        "canon": "directly supported by the books",
        "implied": "strongly supported inference",
        "reconstructed": "practical fill where canon is silent"
    }
}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

def save_floor(name, data):
    txt = json.dumps(data, indent=2)
    (examples_dir / f'{name}.json').write_text(txt, encoding='utf-8')
    (floors_dir / f'{name}.json').write_text(txt, encoding='utf-8')

(tools_dir / 'validate_floor_data_v2.py').write_text("""
import json
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict
FLOOR_DIR = Path("./examples")
def load_jsons():
    return [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in FLOOR_DIR.glob("*.json")]
def bbox(poly):
    xs = [p["x"] for p in poly]; ys = [p["y"] for p in poly]
    return min(xs), min(ys), max(xs), max(ys)
def validate_ids(data):
    seen = set(); problems = []
    for group in ["points","walls","doors","stairs","windows","rooms","hotspots"]:
        for item in data.get(group, []):
            i = item["id"]
            if i in seen: problems.append(f"Duplicate id: {i}")
            seen.add(i)
    return problems
def validate_refs(data):
    problems = []
    points = {p["id"] for p in data.get("points", [])}
    walls = {w["id"] for w in data.get("walls", [])}
    rooms = {r["id"] for r in data.get("rooms", [])}
    for w in data.get("walls", []):
        if w["a"] not in points or w["b"] not in points: problems.append(f'Wall {w["id"]} bad point ref')
    for d in data.get("doors", []):
        if d["wall_id"] not in walls: problems.append(f'Door {d["id"]} bad wall ref')
    for w in data.get("windows", []):
        if w["wall_id"] not in walls: problems.append(f'Window {w["id"]} bad wall ref')
    for h in data.get("hotspots", []):
        if h["room_id"] not in rooms: problems.append(f'Hotspot {h["id"]} bad room ref')
    return problems
def validate_rooms(data):
    fx1, fy1, fx2, fy2 = bbox(data["footprint"])
    probs = []
    for r in data.get("rooms", []):
        rx1, ry1, rx2, ry2 = bbox(r["polygon"])
        if rx1 < fx1 or ry1 < fy1 or rx2 > fx2 or ry2 > fy2:
            probs.append(f'Room {r["id"]} exceeds footprint')
    return probs
def validate_stairs(datas):
    by_building = defaultdict(list)
    for d in datas: by_building[d["building"]].append(d)
    probs = []
    for b, floors in by_building.items():
        names = defaultdict(list)
        for f in floors:
            for s in f.get("stairs", []):
                names[s["name"]].append((f["floor"], round(s["x"],2), round(s["y"],2)))
        for name, items in names.items():
            if len(items) > 1:
                xs = {i[1] for i in items}; ys = {i[2] for i in items}
                if len(xs) > 1 or len(ys) > 1:
                    probs.append(f"{b}: stair '{name}' misaligned: {items}")
    return probs
def main():
    datas = load_jsons(); probs = []
    for d in datas:
        probs += [f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_ids(d)]
        probs += [f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_refs(d)]
        probs += [f'{d["id"]}: {p}' for p in validate_rooms(d)]
    probs += validate_stairs(datas)
    if probs:
        print("VALIDATION PROBLEMS:")
        for p in probs: print("-", p)
    else:
        print("All validations passed.")
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
""".strip(), encoding='utf-8')

(godot_dir / 'floor_visibility_manager.gd').write_text("""
extends Node
func show_only_floor(building_root: Node, floor_name: String) -> void:
    for child in building_root.get_children():
        child.visible = (child.name == floor_name)
func show_all_floors(building_root: Node) -> void:
    for child in building_root.get_children():
        child.visible = true
""".strip(), encoding='utf-8')

(godot_dir / 'hotspot_panel.gd').write_text("""
extends Control
@onready var title_label = $MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/Title
@onready var meta_label = $MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/Meta
@onready var text_label = $MarginContainer/VBoxContainer/Text
func show_hotspot(data: Dictionary) -> void:
    visible = true
    title_label.text = data.get("title", "")
    meta_label.text = "%s | %s | %s" % [data.get("building", ""), data.get("floor", ""), data.get("certainty", "")]
    text_label.text = data.get("lore_text", "")
func hide_panel() -> void:
    visible = false
""".strip(), encoding='utf-8')

extends Control
signal building_selected(building_id: String)
signal floor_selected(floor_id: String)
func populate_buildings(_manifest: Dictionary) -> void:
    pass
func emit_building(id: String) -> void:
    emit_signal("building_selected", id)
func emit_floor(id: String) -> void:
    emit_signal("floor_selected", id)
""".strip(), encoding='utf-8')

(godot_dir / 'north_compass.gd').write_text("""
extends Control
@export var player_path: NodePath
var player: Node3D
func _ready() -> void:
    if player_path != NodePath():
        player = get_node(player_path)
func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
    if player:
        rotation = -player.rotation.y
""".strip(), encoding='utf-8')
